- `ArmourWearMobMixin` [18] `src/main/java/com/jaymospowers/mixin/ArmourWearMobMixin.java`: An NPC off the battlefield mends its worn armour (ArmourWear#rest).
- `ArmourWearRepairMixin` [22] `src/main/java/com/jaymospowers/mixin/ArmourWearRepairMixin.java`: No armour repairs at an anvil in the middle of a fight (ArmourWear#refuseRepair).
- `ArmourWearStackMixin` [52] `src/main/java/com/jaymospowers/mixin/ArmourWearStackMixin.java`: An armour piece worn to 0 stays, broken and protecting nothing, until it is repaired (ArmourWear).
- `AttackTellBrainMixin` [22] `src/main/java/com/jaymospowers/mixin/AttackTellBrainMixin.java`: A brain-driven mob's melee blow (piglins, hoglins, zoglins) winds up with a glint first (see AttackTells).
- `AttackTellGoalMixin` [22] `src/main/java/com/jaymospowers/mixin/AttackTellGoalMixin.java`: A goal-driven mob's melee blow winds up with a glint first (see AttackTells).
- `BackpackExplosionMixin` [39] `src/main/java/com/jaymospowers/mixin/BackpackExplosionMixin.java`: A strong explosion that breaks blocks tears apart the set-down backpacks in its reach (Backpack.blast).
- `BlastGoreMixin` [23] `src/main/java/com/jaymospowers/mixin/BlastGoreMixin.java`: While a vanilla blast hurts things, BlastGore knows where it went off and how big it is (how close each body was).
- `CarryLivingEntityMixin` [40] `src/main/java/com/jaymospowers/mixin/CarryLivingEntityMixin.java`: A carried body shares its carrier's spot: they don't shove each other apart, and it's out of the way of crosshairs and projectiles (the carrier's own arrows would hit it...
- `CarryPacketListenerMixin` [33] `src/main/java/com/jaymospowers/mixin/CarryPacketListenerMixin.java`: A carried (or just let go) player goes where the carry puts them: never kicked for floating or moving too fast.
- `CarryPlayerMixin` [21] `src/main/java/com/jaymospowers/mixin/CarryPlayerMixin.java`: A carried player is out of the way of crosshairs and projectiles too (Player overrides isPickable).
- `CauldronBrewItemMixin` [32] `src/main/java/com/jaymospowers/mixin/CauldronBrewItemMixin.java`: Items thrown into a water cauldron go to ore washing, else to cauldron brewing (CauldronBrewing#itemInside).
- `CostumeNameMixin` [26] `src/main/java/com/jaymospowers/mixin/CostumeNameMixin.java`: A player whose costume hides their face goes by the costume's name (SecretIdentity) wherever their display name shows: chat, death and join messages, the name over their...
- `CostumeTabListMixin` [22] `src/main/java/com/jaymospowers/mixin/CostumeTabListMixin.java`: The tab list shows a masked player by their costume's name (SecretIdentity#update resends it on a change).
- `CrawlJumpMixin` [21] `src/main/java/com/jaymospowers/mixin/CrawlJumpMixin.java`: A crawling player never hops: Jump is how you get up (the client ends the crawl first when there's room).
- `CrawlPlayerMixin` [23] `src/main/java/com/jaymospowers/mixin/CrawlPlayerMixin.java`: A player crawling by the gesture lies flat (the swimming pose, as vanilla crawling under a low ceiling uses);
- `CrowdWaitMixin` [27] `src/main/java/com/jaymospowers/mixin/CrowdWaitMixin.java`: A goal-driven foe waiting its turn (see Crowds) doesn't close in or strike: Crowds walks it round the ring.
- `DismemberMobMixin` [20] `src/main/java/com/jaymospowers/mixin/DismemberMobMixin.java`: A beheaded mob staggering about has no mind left to run its AI (see Dismember#stagger).
- `DownedHealMixin` [32] `src/main/java/com/jaymospowers/mixin/DownedHealMixin.java`: The downed don't heal, from anything: no meal (Kitchen's stomach), no power, no potion.
- `DownedLimboMixin` [28] `src/main/java/com/jaymospowers/mixin/DownedLimboMixin.java`: A blow that left a player at no health with their death turned away (a save that forgot to give any health back) is caught as it lands: they go down, or die properly...
- `DownedMobMixin` [26] `src/main/java/com/jaymospowers/mixin/DownedMobMixin.java`: (0.14.1, from Kingdoms' 0.7.73) A downed mob (Downed) has no AI (goals, brain, pathing): it gets up, bleeds out, or crawls.
- `DroppedArmsDeathMixin` [21] `src/main/java/com/jaymospowers/mixin/DroppedArmsDeathMixin.java`: A mob killed outright mostly lets go of its weapons as it dies (DroppedArms), before its loot and corpse.
- `ExplosionLogMixin` [18] `src/main/java/com/jaymospowers/mixin/ExplosionLogMixin.java`: (BWM port) Logs each blast a player is behind as it goes off (ExplosionLog).
- `FistGuardInventoryMixin` [45] `src/main/java/com/jaymospowers/mixin/FistGuardInventoryMixin.java`: FistGuard: mid-fight, the free slot a pickup gets is never your empty selected one. Methods: getSelectedSlot
- `GuardOverTalkMixin` [30] `src/main/java/com/jaymospowers/mixin/GuardOverTalkMixin.java`: Right click on a foe with a guarding weapon raises the guard instead of opening the foe's chat (MCA's orcs, guards and bandits override interact themselves) or trades:...

- `HammerChargeBlockMixin` [24] `src/main/java/com/jaymospowers/mixin/HammerChargeBlockMixin.java`: A warhammer carries the weapon guard (combat/Guard) for while its smash cools down;
- `IchorclothMagicMixin` [19] `src/main/java/com/jaymospowers/mixin/IchorclothMagicMixin.java`: Ichorcloth turns aside part of every magic blow (AlchemyGear#magicTaken), after vanilla's own magic protection.
- `ImpactShakeMixin` [31] `src/main/java/com/jaymospowers/mixin/ImpactShakeMixin.java`: Landings and heavy footsteps shake the screen (see Impacts).
- `LeavesEntityMixin` : Leaves.Dragged [36] `src/main/java/com/jaymospowers/mixin/LeavesEntityMixin.java`: Remembers the last tick leaves dragged on this entity, so a body in several leaves blocks is dragged once.
- `LeavesJumperMixin` : Leaves.Jumper [19] `src/main/java/com/jaymospowers/mixin/LeavesJumperMixin.java`: Lets Leaves see whether a living thing is holding Jump (to climb up through leaves).
- `LeavesPathMixin` [27] `src/main/java/com/jaymospowers/mixin/LeavesPathMixin.java`: Leaves are soft (Leaves), so mobs path through them as through open air: they wade through hedges and walk out of bushes, and never plan a walk across the top of leaves...

- `LedgePacketListenerMixin` [33] `src/main/java/com/jaymospowers/mixin/LedgePacketListenerMixin.java`: A player hanging from a ledge (or pulling up over it, or just off it) is never kicked for floating or moving too fast.
- `LivingEntityVoiceAccessor` (interface) [16] `src/main/java/com/jaymospowers/mixin/LivingEntityVoiceAccessor.java`: A body's own hurt cry, from outside it (a downed mob groans with it: DownedCrawl).
- `MobGoalsAccessor` (interface) [14] `src/main/java/com/jaymospowers/mixin/MobGoalsAccessor.java`: A mob's goals, from outside it (the future's gunners give up their melee rush: FutureGuns).
- `MobWeaponDropMixin` [43] `src/main/java/com/jaymospowers/mixin/MobWeaponDropMixin.java`: A weapon looted off a mob (a drowned's trident, a skeleton's bow, a zombie's sword or axe) is nearly always worn out: #WORN_CHANCE of the time it drops with at most...

- `DarktideAirMixin` [21] `src/main/java/com/jaymospowers/power/darktide/mixin/DarktideAirMixin.java`: A Darktide user never runs out of air (both sides, so no bubbles flicker on the HUD).

- `DarktideVisibilityMixin` [32] `src/main/java/com/jaymospowers/power/darktide/mixin/DarktideVisibilityMixin.java`: Veil: mobs only notice a veiled Darktide user within the veil's range (their follow range is scaled down to it).
